# Commands · M · part 7

1,420 entries, mission to modularize. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mission**: Run full attune lifecycle as a mission with state detection and phase routing ([athola/claude-night-market](https://github.com/athola/claude-night-market/blob/HEAD/plugins/attune/commands/mission.md) · ★350)
- **mission**: List active missions with their constraints and alerts ([kornia/bubbaloop](https://github.com/kornia/bubbaloop/blob/HEAD/bubbaloop-mcp/commands/mission.md) · ★30)
- **mission**: Use when work must be provably done, not just finished: governed missions with a durable .ai/ ledger, worker attestation, evidence-gated status, provenance, collision-aware wave planning, agent registry, and process ownership. Triggers: parallel or multi-agent execution, 'is this actually integrated?', 'prove the tests passed cleanly', publishing to a shared branch, reconstructing state after context loss, orphaned dev servers, 'probably pre-existing' claims. Do NOT use for: a single trivial edit (just make it), running quality gates on a diff (use /verify), or building a feature from a PRD (… ([samibs/skillfoundry](https://github.com/samibs/skillfoundry/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mission.md) · ★12)

- **mkg-orchestrate**: Memory-grounded multiagent orchestration. Recall prior MKG learnings, deploy subagents to execute a task in verified phases while routing memory into each one, then capture durable new learnings back to the graph. Use to execute, run, or carry out a task or plan with the Meta Knowledge Graph in the loop. ([neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph](https://github.com/neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph/blob/HEAD/commands/mkg-orchestrate.md) · ★38 · 2 copies)
- **mkg-skill-review**: Human-in-the-loop skill publishing for the Meta Knowledge Graph. Walk through the distilled skill proposals waiting in the review queue, validate each against its source learnings, the safety screen, and the live skill it patches, then publish, fix, or discard it through project_resolve_skill. Use when MKG_SKILL_ACTIVATION=human, when session start says skill proposals await review, or when the user asks to review, approve, or publish skills. ([neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph](https://github.com/neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph/blob/HEAD/commands/mkg-skill-review.md) · ★38 · 2 copies)
- **mkg-start**: Onboard a new MKG agent. Check that the meta-knowledge-graph MCP is mounted and which tools are live, inspect the graph state, then either launch the RoadFlex sales demo or build a custom agent persona by capturing user memories. ([neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph](https://github.com/neo4j-labs/meta-knowledge-graph/blob/HEAD/commands/mkg-start.md) · ★38 · 2 copies)
- **mkpart**: **a `/bin` program.** ([eveningworks/saunaos](https://github.com/eveningworks/saunaos/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/mkpart.md) · ★1)
- **mkr-adr**: Write a numbered ADR for a substantial or hard-to-reverse decision, in the existing docs/adr/NNNN-*.md shape. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-adr.md))
- **mkr-audit**: Run phase 9's ground step - spawn mkr-auditor against a spec's acceptance criteria and a real commit, and record a grounding-audit verdict. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-audit.md))
- **mkr-code-review**: Run G4's audit - spawn mkr-code-reviewer and mkr-security-reviewer against the current diff and record a verdict (phase 7 of the AIDLC loop). ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-code-review.md))
- **mkr-design**: Run phase 3's G3 gate - spawn mkr-design-reviewer and mkr-architecture-reviewer against an ACCEPTED spec's §6/§7/§8, and record a design-gate verdict. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-design.md))
- **mkr-detect**: Read a target repo's root for Node/TypeScript, Python, Go, and Rails ecosystem signals and report a proposed Stack description plus proposed MKR_TEST/MKR_BUILD/MKR_LINT/MKR_TYPECHECK/MKR_COVERAGE values. Never writes anything. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-detect.md))
- **mkr-init**: Interview this repo and seed its CLAUDE.md and .mkr/config from seed/CLAUDE.md and seed/config. Refuses to run against mkr-aidlc's own root, and refuses to silently overwrite an already-filled owned pair. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-init.md))
- **mkr-merge**: Run phase 8's G5 preflight - check G4/CI/spec status and ask MKR_GATE_MERGE's named approver before merging the current branch. Never merges unprompted. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-merge.md))
- **mkr-plan**: Check a presented plan against MKR_PLAN_MANDATORY/MKR_PLAN_OPTIONAL and return CONFORMANT or BLOCKED (phase 2 of the AIDLC loop, G2). ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-plan.md))
- **mkr-rkp**: Build or refresh the Repo Knowledge Package (docs/rkp/) - a refreshable, grounded knowledge-transfer doc set. Derives which doc topics apply per-repo from a signal, never a fixed list. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-rkp.md))
- **mkr-ship**: Run phase 10's G6 gate - check MKR_DEPLOY, and if configured, state the pre-flight and ask before running it. Never auto-deploys. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-ship.md))
- **mkr-spec**: Draft a spec for the change just triaged (phase 1 of the AIDLC loop). Requires a completed mkr-loop triage first. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-spec.md))
- **mkr-spec-review**: Run G1's audit - invoke mkr-spec-reviewer against a spec and record its verdict (phase 1 of the AIDLC loop). ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-spec-review.md))
- **mkr-update**: Run install.sh --dry-run against a source checkout, show a human-readable drift report, and ask before applying the update for real. Never applies unprompted. ([kikrgbh/mkrdlc](https://github.com/kikrgbh/mkrdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mkr-update.md))

- **mm-check-deliverability**: 请检查当前数学建模 run 是否已经达到可交付状态。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-check-deliverability.md) · ★2)
- **mm-context-safe-loop**: 用途：专门解决 VS Code 插件版 Claude Code 在数学建模工作台中长流程推进时的上下文控制问题。该命令每轮只恢复必要状态、执行一个有边界的阶段动作，并把事实写回文件。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-context-safe-loop.md) · ★2)

- **mm-docs**: Look up Mattermost or mautrix documentation using the mattermost-expert agent. ([aiku/mautrix-mattermost](https://github.com/aiku/mautrix-mattermost/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-docs.md))
- **mm-generate-full-pdf**: 目标：在当前数学建模 run 中推进到完整 PDF 论文交付。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-generate-full-pdf.md) · ★2)
- **mm-import-office**: 用途：当 Claude Code 对话框提示 `.xlsx`、`.docx`、`.xls`、`.doc` 等 Office 文件是 unsupported file type 时，用本地转换流程把文件变成可读取的 Markdown/CSV。该命令只做接入层导入说明和路径读取，不创建真实 run，不推进 workflow，不触发升级。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-import-office.md) · ★2)
- **mm-recover-next**: 请从默认 run 的下一步提示词恢复当前数学建模项目： ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-recover-next.md) · ★2)
- **mm-review**: Slash shortcut for adversarial multimodel review of the previous agent's work. ([joi-lab/cursor-multimodel-review](https://github.com/joi-lab/cursor-multimodel-review/blob/HEAD/commands/mm-review.md) · ★16)
- **mm-start-workbench**: 用途：让 VS Code 插件版 Claude Code 在打开 `数学建模智能体/` 作为工作区根目录后，安全识别工作台、恢复默认 run，并决定下一步。该命令只做接入层启动与状态恢复，不创建真实项目，不触发升级。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-start-workbench.md) · ★2)
- **mm-upgrade-explicit**: 仅当用户在当前对话中明确说出“升级”“Phase 26”“upgrade-report”或“升级日报”时，才允许使用本提示词。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-upgrade-explicit.md) · ★2)
- **mm-validate-access-layer-readonly**: 用途：对“数学建模智能体”VS Code + Claude Code 接入层做静态、只读、一致性验证。该命令返回固定 10 个二级标题的审计报告，不额外添加总结标题。该命令不是普通建模、PDF 生成、交付确认或升级执行流程，不推进任何 run，不执行任何会产生副作用的 CLI、slash command、skill 或 VS Code Task。 ([3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent](https://github.com/3412809930-bot/math-modeling-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mm-validate-access-layer-readonly.md) · ★2)

- **mnx-doctor**: Validate the Mnemex graph against all integrity invariants (edge targets, index/node match, denormalized copies, reverse-map, dangling edges, hot bound, cross-links, config drift) and optionally self-heal derived files. ([kritird/OpenMnemex](https://github.com/kritird/OpenMnemex/blob/HEAD/commands/mnx-doctor.md) · ★2)
- **mnx-ingest**: Bootstrap or update a Mnemex knowledge graph from an EXISTING code or documentation repository (a local folder or a git remote) — no live session needed. Walks the source, distills durable atoms (never transcribes), discovers a deduped entity catalog, wikifies links, and stages a labeled bulk batch that /mnemex:mnx-promote --bulk then merges. Two gates only (scope up front, bulk summary at the end); idempotent on re-run. Never writes the graph (staging only) and never mutates the source. ([kritird/OpenMnemex](https://github.com/kritird/OpenMnemex/blob/HEAD/commands/mnx-ingest.md) · ★2)
- **mnx-init**: Set up Mnemex — bind this project (or your user account) to a knowledge-graph repo, or scaffold a brand-new graph. - **mnx-promote**: Promote the locally-staged captures into the shared Mnemex graph — flush usage stamps, reconcile + merge every staged atom (clean-context sub-agent, human-in-the-loop on contradictions), consolidate the post-merge graph, run the doctor, push, and clear staging. Atomic and total; the deliberate `git push`/PR half of memory. ([kritird/OpenMnemex](https://github.com/kritird/OpenMnemex/blob/HEAD/commands/mnx-promote.md) · ★2)

- **mnx-status**: Show Mnemex at a glance — what graph is bound, its kind, node/tier counts per team, pending usage stamps, last gc, and a health summary. Read-only; never syncs or repairs. ([kritird/OpenMnemex](https://github.com/kritird/OpenMnemex/blob/HEAD/commands/mnx-status.md) · ★2)
